      "title": "DeepSeek-V4 详解:1.6T MoE、CSAHCA 混合 attention、mHC 残差稳定,开源 LLM 的下一代旗舰(DeepSeek 系列第 17 篇 · 收官)",
      "link": "https://yudonglee.me/deepseek-v4-explained",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek-V4 详解:1.6T MoE､CSA+HCA 混合 attention､mHC 残差稳定,开源 LLM 的下一代旗舰(DeepSeek 系列第 17 篇 · 收官)｡ 相对同期闭源 frontier 模型(GPT-5.5 估计 $200M+､Claude Opus 4.6 估计 $150M+),V4-Pro 的 $14-18M 成本仍然是 1/10 量级｡ 七､评测全景:SWE-bench 80.6% 与同期 frontier 对齐 7.1 综合能力 V4-Pro 在 2026-04 发布时的主流 benchmark: Benchmark DeepSeek V4-Pro Claude Opus 4.6 GPT-5.5 Gemini 3.1 Pro MMLU-Pro 89.5 89.0 90.2 89.3 GPQA-Diamond 79.1 81.0 80.5 78.5 MATH-500 98.5 96.0 97.5 98.0 AIME 2025 88.0 80.3 85.0 86.5 HumanEval 94.5 95.5 96.0 94.0 SWE-bench Verified 80.6 82.0 79.8 81.5 LiveCodeBench 68.5 70.0 67.5 69.0 V4-Pro 在大多数 benchmark 上与三个闭源旗舰处于**±2% 范围内**｡在数学(AIME 88.0 vs 80-86)上有明显优势——继承自 W15 Math-V2 的 self-verifiable reasoning 方法论｡ 7.2 SWE-bench Verified:agentic coding 的标志性指标 SWE-bench Verified 是 2025-2026 年 frontier 模型最重要的 benchmark——它测试模型是否能自主修复真实 GitHub repository 中的 bug: 输入:完整 repo + issue 描述 模型必须 explore repo､定位 bug､写出 patch､跑测试验证 Verified split 经过人工严格筛选,确保题目可解 这是真正的 agentic 任务——需要长上下文(看整个 repo)+ tool use(grep / read file / run tests)+ multi-step planning｡ V4-Pro 的 80.6% 在 2026-04 是开源模型 SOTA,与 Claude Opus 4.6 (82.0) 仅差 1.4pt｡",

      "snippet": "DeepSeek V4-Pro Review: GA, Pricing & Benchmarks Codersera Blogs｡ reach for Claude Opus 4.7 on the longest agentic loops and GPT-5.5 for multimodal work. DeepSeek V4-Pro is the flagship of the DeepSeek V4 family, released April 24, 2026 by DeepSeek (Hangzhou-based; the same lab behind V3 and the R1 reasoning series). It is a 1.6 trillion parameter Mixture-of-Experts model that activates 49 billion parameters per token, putting per-token inference cost close to a 49B dense model while retaining the knowledge capacity of a much larger one. Two facts make V4-Pro distinct from prior open-weight efforts: Hybrid attention. V4 combines Compressed Sparse Attention (CSA) with Heavily Compressed Attention (HCA) so the model can serve a 1M-token context at roughly 27% of V3.2's per-token FLOPs and 10% of its KV-cache memory. This is what makes long-context production deployments financially viable. Domestic training stack.       "title": "DeepSeek V4-Pro: 1.6T MoE, 1M Context, Frontier Coding",
      "link": "https://deepseekai.guide/?p=241/",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek V4-Pro: 1.6T MoE, 1M Context, Frontier Coding DeepSeek AI Guide｡ They are DeepSeek-reported evaluations under the company’s own configurations; independent replication will take days to weeks, as with every major release. Benchmark V4-Pro (reasoning_effort=max) --- --- SWE-Bench Verified 80.6 Terminal-Bench 2.0 67.9 LiveCodeBench 93.5 Codeforces rating 3206 HMMT 2026 95.2 IMOAnswerBench 89.8 HLE (no tools) 37.7 SimpleQA-Verified 57.9 MMLU-Pro 87.5 GPQA Diamond 90.1 MRCR 1M (long context) 83.5 Sources: On coding benchmarks, DeepSeek V4-Pro leads Claude on Terminal-Bench 2.0 (67.9% vs 65.4%), LiveCodeBench (93.5% vs 88.8%), and Codeforces rating (3206 vs no reported score). Claude Opus 4.6 holds a marginal lead on SWE-bench Verified (80.8% vs 80.6%), and a meaningful lead on HLE (40.0% vs 37.7%) and HMMT 2026 math (96.2% vs 95.2%). GPQA Diamond 90.1, GSM8K 92.6, HLE 37.7, MMLU-Pro 87.5, SWE-Bench Pro 55.4, SWE-Bench Verified 80.6. Codeforces 3206 is the line that matters. That is better than GPT-5.4 (xHigh) at 3168. MRCR 1M: 83.5 vs Opus 4.6’s 92.9. Opus is still the long-context leader. You can cross-check individual leaderboard entries at the official SWE-bench leaderboards, and the full DeepSeek V4-Pro model card on Hugging Face. Strengths Agentic coding. V4-Pro at 67.9% on Terminal-Bench 2.0 beats Claude Opus 4.6 (65.4%) by 2.5 points — this benchmark involves real autonomous terminal execution with a 3-hour timeout. That gap matters for agentic workflows more than a single-turn coding benchmark would. Competitive programming. The Codeforces rating of 3206 puts V4-Pro ahead of GPT-5.4 in that specific style of reasoning. Formal and olympiad math. V4-Pro beats Claude and Gemini on IMOAnswerBench and lands close to GPT-5.4 on HMMT 2026. Long-context economics. The 10% KV cache figure vs V3.2 is the reason 1M context ships as default, not as a premium add-on. Licensing clarity. Both code and weights are MIT, which simplifies commercial use compared with the separate DeepSeek Model License that covered some older releases (V3 base, Coder-V2, VL2). Weaknesses World knowledge. SimpleQA-Verified at 57.9% versus Gemini’s 75.6% reveals a meaningful factual knowledge retrieval gap. If your use case requires accurate real-world knowledge recall — not just code generation — Gemini holds a clear edge. Long-context retrieval quality. V4-Pro ships the infrastructure to process 1M tokens cheaply, but Opus 4.6 still extracts more accurately from very long haystacks on MRCR. HLE. Gemini 3.1 Pro pulls ahead on cross-domain expert reasoning. Tooling caveat. This release does not include a Jinja-format chat template. DeepSeek provides Python encoding scripts in the model repository for prompt construction. Plan for this in your integration. Self-hosting hardware. At 865 GB on disk, V4-Pro realistically needs a multi-GPU rack; most developers will reach for V4-Flash for local work. How to access V4-Pro Web and app On chat.deepseek.com and the mobile app, V4-Pro is exposed as Expert Mode; V4-Flash appears as Instant Mode. The DeepThink toggle switches between non-thinking and thinking modes on whichever tier you have selected. See the DeepSeek chat walkthrough if you are new to the interface. API Chat requests hit POST /chat/completions, the OpenAI-compatible endpoint at https://api.deepseek.com. DeepSeek also ships an Anthropic-compatible surface against the same base URL, so the Anthropic SDK works by swapping base_url and api_key. The API is stateless — your client must resend the full conversation history on every request. The web and app, by contrast, keep session state for you.",

    },

      "snippet": "DeepSeek V4 Pro API WaveSpeedAI｡ process.exitCode = 1; } Model Introduction Deepseek deepseek-v4-pro DeepSeek-V4-Pro is DeepSeek's most powerful open-source model, released on April 24, 2026.       "title": "DeepSeek V4",
      "link": "https://howaiworks.ai/models/deepseek",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek V4 HowAIWorks.ai｡ Competitive and algorithmic programming: A 3206 Codeforces rating and 93.5 on LiveCodeBench make V4-Pro a strong assistant for algorithm design and contest-style problems. Long-document analysis: Legal discovery, regulatory filings, and multi-paper literature review, where a million tokens of context removes most chunking and retrieval plumbing. Cost-sensitive high-volume workloads: V4-Flash delivers the same context and output limits at roughly a third of Pro's input price and a third of its output price. Private and air-gapped deployment: MIT-licensed weights make on-premises hosting viable for regulated industries. Agentic pipelines: Long outputs and hybrid thinking support multi-step tool-using agents without a separate reasoning model. Performance / Benchmarks Numbers below are from DeepSeek's own model cards on Hugging Face. DeepSeek-V4-Pro Benchmark Score SWE-bench Verified 80.6 GPQA Diamond 90.1 LiveCodeBench 93.5 Codeforces (rating) 3206 MMLU-Pro 87.5 Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) 37.7 DeepSeek-V4-Flash Benchmark Score LiveCodeBench 91.6 MMLU-Pro 86.2 SimpleQA-Verified 34.1 MRCR (1M long context) 78.7 V4-Flash figures are reported in Think Max mode. Vendor-reported benchmarks are self-selected; treat them as an upper bound and validate on your own evaluation set. Limitations No single \"V4\" endpoint: You must choose deepseek-v4-pro or deepseek-v4-flash. Code that assumes a generic deepseek-v4 ID will fail. Preview checkpoint: DeepSeek describes the released V4-Pro weights as \"a preview version\" of the series. Behavior may shift in later revisions. Legacy ID cutoff: deepseek-chat and deepseek-reasoner stop working on July 24, 2026. Serving cost at 1.6T: Even at 49B active parameters, self-hosting V4-Pro requires a substantial multi-GPU cluster. V4-Flash is the practical self-hosting target for most teams. Weights, not full openness: MIT covers the weights. Training data and the full training pipeline are not published. No published knowledge cutoff: DeepSeek does not state a reliable knowledge cutoff date for V4. Ground time-sensitive queries with retrieval or tool use. Benchmarks are vendor-reported: Independent third-party replication of the headline scores is limited. Pricing & Access Prices are from DeepSeek's pricing page, quoted per 1M tokens in USD. Model Input (cache hit) Input (cache miss) Output deepseek-v4-pro $0.003625 $0.435 $0.87 deepseek-v4-flash $0.0028 $0.14 $0.28 The cache-hit price is roughly 1/120th of the cache-miss price on V4-Pro, which makes prompt caching the single highest-leverage optimization for any repeated-context workload. DeepSeek does not publish an off-peak discount for the V4 models. Access options: DeepSeek API — OpenAI ChatCompletions and Anthropic Messages interfaces DeepSeek Chat — official web and mobile applications Hugging Face — MIT-licensed weights for private deployment Ecosystem & Tools DeepSeek API documentation — reference, changelog, and migration guides DeepSeek-V4-Pro on Hugging Face — frontier-tier weights DeepSeek-V4-Flash on Hugging Face — efficient-tier weights DeepSeek-V4-Pro-DSpark — the same V4-Pro checkpoint with a speculative decoding module for vLLM GitHub — inference code and research releases vLLM — supported serving path, including DSpark speculative decoding behind a single flag Community & Resources DeepSeek API changelog — the V4-Pro / V4-Flash launch entry and the legacy-ID deprecation notice Pricing DeepSeek-V4-Pro model card DeepSeek on GitHub Compare with Kimi K2.6, GLM-5.2, Qwen3.7-Max, and Ling-2.6-1T Frequently Asked Questions When was DeepSeek V4 released? DeepSeek V4 arrived on April 24, 2026, when the DeepSeek API changelog announced that \"The DeepSeek API now supports V4-Pro and V4-Flash, available via both the OpenAI ChatCompletions interface and the Anthropic interface.\" Is there a model called just \"DeepSeek V4\"? No. \"DeepSeek V4\" is the series name. Two models shipped: deepseek-v4-pro and deepseek-v4-flash. There is no callable deepseek-v4 model ID. What is the difference between V4-Pro and V4-Flash?",

      "snippet": "DeepSeek V4 Deep Dive: DSA Sparse Attention, 1M Context, and a Full Comparison with GPT-5 / Claude / Gemini｡ red is a clear gap. Benchmark DeepSeek V4-Pro Claude Opus 4.6 GPT-5.4 Gemini 3.1-Pro LiveCodeBench 93.5 88.8 — 91.7 Codeforces Rating 3,206 — 3,168 — SWE-bench Verified 80.6 80.8 — 80.6 MMLU-Pro 87.5 89.1 87.5 91.0 GPQA Diamond 90.1 91.3 93.0 94.3 Terminal-Bench 2.0 67.9 65.4 75.1 68.5 HLE (Hard Logic Eval) 37.7 — — — How to read this Coding and competitive programming: V4-Pro is the top open-source result and ties or beats closed-source frontiers. LiveCodeBench 93.5 leads both GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1-Pro, and Codeforces 3206 edges out GPT-5.4. SWE-bench Verified is a three-way tie with Claude Opus 4.6 and Gemini 3.1-Pro. Knowledge-heavy and scientific reasoning are still gaps. MMLU-Pro trails Gemini by 3.5 points; GPQA Diamond trails by 4.2 points. DeepSeek itself acknowledges a \"3–6 month lag\" behind the SOTA frontier on these. Command-line agentic work (Terminal-Bench): V4 is on par with Claude/Gemini, but materially behind GPT-5.4. GPT-5.4's 75.1 remains the single best score for tool-using agents. The conclusion is clean: V4-Pro is the open-source frontier, but not yet the absolute frontier. If your workload is centered on coding, algorithms, or long-document handling, V4-Pro is performance-equivalent and an order of magnitude cheaper. If you need top-tier scientific reasoning, closed-source flagships still have the edge — but the gap is narrowing. 5. Pricing: a structural cost shift Model Input ($/1M tokens) Output ($/1M tokens) Context License DeepSeek V4-Pro 1.74 3.48 1M MIT (open) DeepSeek V4-Flash 0.14 0.28 1M MIT (open) OpenAI GPT-5.4 ~10 ~30 200K Closed Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6 ~7.5 ~25 500K Closed Moonshot Kimi (latest) ~1.5 ~4 200K Closed API Alibaba Qwen-3.5-Max ~0.8 ~2.4 1M Mixed (mid-size open) V4-Pro's output price is roughly 9× cheaper than OpenAI and 7× cheaper than Claude. V4-Flash output at $0.28/M is approximately 1/100 of OpenAI's. Even within the Chinese open-source camp, V4-Flash undercuts Qwen-3.5-Max by 8–9×. This isn't a promotional price. Combined with the architecture changes above, V4's pricing is structural — MoE activation efficiency + DSA-driven KV reduction + Ascend 950 inference optimization stack into a real per-token cost reduction. Even if OpenAI or Anthropic respond with price cuts, matching this floor is hard. 6. Selection guide: when to use V4 — and when not to V4-Pro is the natural choice when… ✓ Large-codebase refactors, full-repo reviews, multi-file edits: 1M context plus top-tier LiveCodeBench/SWE-bench performance — V4-Pro is the most cost-effective \"global code understanding\" model available. ✓ Long-document / PDF / contract analysis: 1M context fits an entire book or dozens of contracts; tokens cost about 1/7 of Claude. ✓ Cost-sensitive Agent orchestration at scale: if your Agent system burns thousands of dollars in tokens daily, V4-Pro can shave roughly 80% off your LLM bill.       "snippet": "DeepSeek Harness开源即爆火,Agent竞争进入执行层 新浪看点｡DeepSeek Harness开源即爆火,Agent竞争进入执行层 ** 8月13日晚,DeepSeek正式发布了首款Agent产品DeepSeek Harness的开发者预览版(v0.1),并以MIT协议全面开源,上线几小时GitHub便拿下3万多颗星标,直接对标OpenAI Codex和Anthropic Claude Code,宣告大模型竞争正式从模型层延伸到\"执行层\"｡ 一､核心发布:一切皆插件的开源Agent框架 时间与形态:2026年8月13日,DeepSeek正式推出Harness开发者预览版,同步以MIT协议开放源代码｡项目负责人崔添翼表示\"目前的版本还很不完善,请大家多提意见\"｡ 架构哲学:采用\"一切皆插件\"的设计思路——模型､工具､会话､沙箱､存储､UI等所有Agent能力均由插件组合而成,可自由替换､灵活重组｡底层基于Cordis插件系统构建,支持热插拔与依赖倒置｡ 四种运行模式:标准模式(完整工具组合)､PTC模式(程序化工具调用)､极简模式(仅bash+文件编辑,用于基准测试)､创造模式(运行时检查与Preset创作)｡ 二､设计定位:从模型提供商到智能体基础设施 核心公式:官方将Harness定义为\"Model + Harness = Agent\",即大模型负责思考推理,Harness承担工具调用､任务规划､执行调度等所有工程化工作｡ 不挑模型:系统不限于DeepSeek自家模型,可接入OpenAI､Anthropic､Kimi､Google等主流大模型｡甚至已能直接调用本机的Claude Code､Codex协同工作｡ 行业对标:直接切入代码智能体赛道,对标Codex和Claude Code｡通过插件化架构,它更像一个\"组装智能体的方式\"而非固定形态的编程助手｡ 三､市场反响:开源狂欢与生态想象力 GitHub热度:发布数小时内星标突破3万,增速惊人｡开发者社群围绕其\"极端插件化\"展开两极讨论:一派盛赞底层架构强,一派抱怨学习门槛高｡ 成本优势显现:有测试显示4轮任务跑127步,输入810万token,缓存命中率达99%,总成本极低｡第三方横向评测认为其单任务推理成本远低于Claude Code｡ 生态意义:补齐了国产大模型从\"提供模型\"到\"提供完整开发工具链\"的关键一环,让国内开发者拥有自主可控的Agent基础设施｡",

    {
      "title": "DeepSeek开源Agent框架Harness v0.1开启全球测试:首创“全插件”架构",
      "link": "https://m.sohu.com/a/1062978920_122396381",
      "snippet": "2026年8月14日,中国人工智能公司DeepSeek(深度求索)正式宣布,其Agent Harness(智能体运行框架)的开发者预览版DeepSeek Harness v0.1现已面向全球开发者开放测试,并采用MIT许可证正式开源｡ 作为早期预览版本,DeepSeek Harness在架构设计上采取了极具创新性的“一切皆插件(everything is a plugin)”理念,通过纯粹的插件化架构来构建Agent Harness｡在该框架下,模型､工具､技能､会话､沙盒､存储､循环､调度以及用户界面(UI)等核心能力,均以独立插件的形式进行封装与组合｡这种设计允许开发者对各项能力进行独立替换与灵活重组｡ 根据Gartner发布的《AI技术成熟度曲线》显示,当前主流AI Agent框架普遍面临组件耦合度高､扩展性受限的痛点｡为解决这一问题,DeepSeek Harness主要基于Cordis(元框架)插件系统构建｡Cordis元框架本身仅负责插件的加载､卸载以及依赖管理｡Agent Harness的具体功能组件则由不同的Cordis插件实现,各插件之间通过服务(service)和事件(event)机制进行高效协作｡这意味着,开发者可以在配置层面独立选择､替换或扩展任何功能,而无需修改DeepSeek Harness的底层源代码,极大降低了二次开发的门槛｡ 针对不同应用场景,DeepSeek Harness提供了四种运行模式: 标准模式(Standard):适用于常规的智能体任务编排与执行｡ PTC模式(编程式工具调用,Programmatic Tool Call):在此模式下,模型会直接生成代码,以组合和执行多轮复杂的工具调用,提升了处理长链路任务的效率｡ 极简模式(Minimal):该模式仅保留Shell和文件编辑工具,专为极简环境下的模型基准测试(Benchmark)而设计｡ 创意模式(Creative):支持开发者检查运行时状态,在内存中直接实验Cordis插件,从而快速组合和验证新的运行模式｡ 在开发者体验方面,只要系统配备了Node.js开发工具链,即可通过npx命令快速启动其Web UI,实现开箱即用｡ 据《麻省理工科技评论》此前报道,DeepSeek一直秉持高性价比与全面开源的策略,此前推出的DeepSeek-V2及DeepSeek-Coder等模型在开源社区获得了广泛关注｡此次DeepSeek Harness v0.1的开源,进一步完善了其在AI Agent基础设施领域的布局｡通过引入高度解耦的插件架构,DeepSeek正试图为全球开发者提供一个更具弹性和可扩展性的智能体开发底座,推动AI Agent技术从实验室走向更广泛的复杂生产环境｡",

      "snippet": "国盛证券:DeepSeek Harness开源发布 全栈插件化构建Agent执行底座 腾讯网｡8月19日,2026年8月13日,DeepSeek Harness以开发者预览版开源,面向全球Harness开发者开放测试｡8月17日,项目发布v0.1.0-rc.7预发布版,新增插件自注册设置卡片,并优化Cordis动态插件面板｡ Harness连接模型､工具与任务环境,补齐模型之外的Agent执行层｡DeepSeek将Harness定位为开源Agent框架,其标准模式支持文件编辑､命令行操作､文件与网页检索､Skills､计划､目标､子Agent和工作流,并通过会话日志记录模型可见内容及工具调用过程｡Harness承接的是“如何让模型持续完成任务的环节”,使模型能力由单次响应进一步延伸至可执行､可记录和可复用的工作流程,推动DeepSeek由模型API走向实际工作交付层｡ 多模型接入与四种运行模式,覆盖从直接使用到深度开发的不同需求｡Harness除支持DeepSeek模型外,也可配置Anthropic､OpenAI等模型提供方,并允许企业通过自定义兼容端点连接内部网关或自建模型服务｡标准模式提供完整工具组合;PTC模式通过一段程序集中编排多步工具调用;极简模式仅保留持续运行的命令行和文件编辑器;创造模式则可检查运行环境､试验Cordis插件并组合新的Agent运行方案｡多模型与多模式使同一框架能够覆盖基础测试､日常任务执行､复杂工具编排和专用Agent开发,拓展Harness的适用范围｡ “一切皆插件”构成Harness区别于一般Agent工具的核心架构优势｡Harness源代码采用MIT许可证,官方将GitHub的dsh-plugin专题作为社区插件发现入口,并支持通过npm或Git分发插件组合包｡第三方社区项目已覆盖图像理解､长期记忆､飞书接入､Token成本统计､桌面客户端和插件管理等方向｡开放的发现和分发机制有助于外部开发者持续补充模型､工具､渠道及行业工作流,推动Harness形成可复用､可扩展的开发者生态｡ 开源､多模型兼容与全栈插件化协同,有望降低个人和企业搭建专属Agent体系的门槛｡Harness允许用户组合外部插件并保留自己的运行配置,Python SDK也可将同一套执行能力嵌入自动化程序和内部业务系统｡",

      "snippet": "杭州深度求索AI3年磨一剑开源DeepSeekHarness,为何说它重构了Agent开发范式? 联索科技｡杭州深度求索AI成立3年开源DeepSeek Harness,4种运行模式重构Agent开发生态杭州深度求索人工智能基础技术研究有限公司(DeepSeek)成立3年之际,于2026年8月13日正式开源DeepSeek Harness开发者预览版,这款全栈插件化Agent框架支持4种运行模式､多模型接入与跨平台兼容,截至8月21日已完成3次版本迭代,吸引1500+开发者参与测试｡作为幻方量化孵化的AI企业,DeepSeek自2023年成立以来始终坚持｢开源开放｣战略,先后推出DeepSeek-Coder代码模型､DeepSeek-R推理模型等开源产品｡此次Harness的发布,标志着公司从单一模型提供商向全栈AI基础设施服务商转型,填补了国内开源Agent执行层框架的空白｡根据官方披露,DeepSeek Harness采用｢一切皆插件｣的架构设计,支持DeepSeek､OpenAI､Anthropic等多模型接入,提供标准模式､PTC模式､极简模式､创造模式4种运行形态,开发者可通过插件扩展实现文件操作､代码执行､网页检索等复杂任务｡截至8月21日,项目已发布v0.1.1-rc.1版本,新增多模态图文输入､子代理Job Panel等核心功能｡为什么DeepSeek Harness被称为｢Agent开发的瑞士军刀｣?DeepSeek Harness之所以被开发者称为｢Agent开发的瑞士军刀｣,核心在于它首次将全栈插件化架构与多模型调度能力深度融合,将Agent开发门槛从｢系统级工程｣降低到｢积木式组装｣｡国盛证券在2026年8月19日发布的《AI产业研究系列报告》中指出:｢DeepSeek Harness开源补齐模型之外的Agent执行层,推动DeepSeek由模型API走向实际工作交付层｡｣报告显示,该框架支持4种运行模式,覆盖从基础功能测试到专用Agent开发的全场景需求:标准模式提供完整工具组合,适合日常任务处理;PTC模式集中编排多步工具调用,适配复杂工作流;极简模式保留核心命令行能力,满足轻量开发需求;创造模式支持插件试验与环境检查,面向进阶开发者｡从技术架构来看,DeepSeek Harness的｢一切皆插件｣设计是其核心竞争力｡开发者可通过npm或Git分发插件组合包,官方GitHub的dsh-plugin专题已收录图像理解､飞书接入､Token统计等第三方插件｡",

      "title": "DeepSeek Open-Sources Harness, an All-Plugin Agent Runtime",
      "link": "http://rits.shanghai.nyu.edu/ai/deepseek-harness-cordis-everything-is-a-plugin",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek Open-Sources Harness, an All-Plugin Agent Runtime 纽约大学上海中心图书馆｡DeepSeek released DeepSeek Harness v0.1 on August 13, 2026 — an open-source agent runtime, MIT-licensed and in developer preview, built on a single architectural bet: there is no privileged core. Models, tools, skills, sessions, sandboxes, filesystems, the agent loop itself, and even the UI are all plugins mounted into a shared context. The framework underneath, Cordis, is not new to this project — it has been running the Koishi chatbot ecosystem for four years — and it shipped the same day as a formal paper co-authored with Peking University giving it a mathematical foundation. Advanced Image credit: DeepSeek Harness What Shipped The harness is distributed as dsh and runs from npm without a clone: npx @deepseek-ai/dsh web That starts a web UI on http://127.0.0.1:3080. Building from source is a standard pnpm workflow (pnpm install, pnpm run build, pnpm dsh web). The repository carries an explicit developer-preview warning about breaking changes, and it has drawn attention accordingly — 72.6k stars and 6.2k forks at the time of writing. Composition happens in layers rather than in code. Bundles ship Cordis config rows plus the code they configure; profiles are named compositions stored in the harness home directory. The layers apply in order — base bundles, then profile patches, then home-level patches, then CLI overlays — and each layer can patch a row by ID, replacing a whole configuration or inserting a new one. dsh --profile web --dump-config prints the resolved tree. The core bundles are dsh-base (model adapters, tools, persistence, sandbox, credentials), dsh-web-app (the browser UI), and dsh-headless (a one-shot runner with no server). Everything Is a Plugin The plugin claim is literal, and the settings panel above is the evidence: 159 plugins in a default deployment, with llm, session, and the hot-module-reload plugin sitting in the same list as everything else, individually toggleable. Capabilities are exposed as services on stable context keys, so a consumer never imports a provider directly: Package Service Context key core/session Append-only event log and store ctx.sessions core/tools Scoped registry and execution ctx.tools core/agent Agent interface and lifecycle ctx.agents core/agent-loop Default driver implementation ctx.agentLoop llm/llm Message vocabulary and adapter seam ctx.llm core/system-prompt Prompt assembly ctx.systemPrompt The documentation calls each of these a seam, with three roles: a service definition (the interface contract), a service provider (the swappable implementation), and a consumer. The payoff is that a single provider swap propagates. Replacing the filesystem provider, the architecture document notes, moves Bash, PTY, and LSP onto a remote sandbox — with no forking of any of them. Turns, Steps, and an Append-Only Log A turn contains zero or more steps, where a step is one model request plus the tool calls it produces. The flow, with its extension points, looks like this: turn/start → agent/pre-step (reject or rewrite messages) → step/start → assemble prompts + schemas → agent/request → llm/stream → assistant/message → tool/call → tools/execute → tool/result → step/end (do tools owe another request?",
      "date": "2026/08/14 00:00:00"
    },

      "title": "DeepSeek Harness 2026:一切皆插件 — 开发者预览版完全指南",
      "link": "https://www.cnblogs.com/sing1ee/p/22455466",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek Harness 2026:一切皆插件 — 开发者预览版完全指南 DeepSeek Harness 2026:一切皆插件 — 开发者预览版完全指南 核心要点 (TL;DR) DeepSeek Harness(CLI 名 dsh)于 2026 年 8 月 13 日向全球 Harness 开发者开放 v0.1 开发者预览版测试,并以 MIT 协议在 GitHub 上开源全部源码｡ DeepSeek Harness 最重要的设计原则是**｢一切皆插件｣**:模型､工具､技能､会话､沙箱､存储､循环､调度､UI 等所有 Agent 能力均由 Cordis 插件组合而成,无需改动源码即可自由替换､灵活重组｡ DeepSeek Harness 提供四种运行模式——标准模式､PTC 模式(程序化工具调用)､极简模式､创造模式,每种模式默认加载不同的插件集合｡ 每一次运行都有迹可循:模型看到的一切都会写入追记式(append-only)会话日志,可在 Trajectory 视图中按来源查看;恢复､分叉､检索与回放共享同一份事件流｡ 一分钟内即可体验 DeepSeek Harness:执行 npx @deepseek-ai/dsh web 即可在 http://127.0.0.1:3080 启动 Web UI｡ 目录 什么是 DeepSeek Harness? ｢一切皆插件｣的设计哲学 DeepSeek Harness 的四种运行模式 追记式会话日志与 Trajectory 视图 架构:Profile､Bundle 与核心包 如何开始使用 DeepSeek Harness 为 DeepSeek Harness 开发插件 DeepSeek Harness 与其他 Agent Harness 对比 常见问题解答 总结 什么是 DeepSeek Harness? DeepSeek Harness 是 DeepSeek AI 开发的开源 Agent Harness｡它位于大语言模型与 Agent 完成任务所需的工具､文件､环境之间,是承载智能体运行的基础设施层｡与许多把组件写死的 Harness 不同,DeepSeek Harness 采取了一种截然不同的思路:每一项能力都是插件,Harness 本身只是插件的组合｡ 2026 年 8 月 13 日,DeepSeek Harness 团队宣布 v0.1 开发者预览版面向全球 Harness 开发者开放测试,并同步以 MIT 协议开放源代码｡团队在官方微信渠道发布的公告中明确表示,v0.1 只是早期预览版本:当前仍有许多细节有待改进和打磨,核心插件与基础接口也将在后续快速迭代演进｡团队正邀请全球 Harness 开发者共同建设 DSH 插件生态｡ 项目位于 github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness,基于 Cordis 插件框架构建,当前仓库版本为 0.1.0-rc.5｡Monorepo 结构包含 apps/(cli 与 web 两个应用)和 packages/(core､llm､mcp､sandbox､context､plan､goal 等数十个包),从中可以直观感受到 DeepSeek Harness 开箱即用的能力广度｡ 专业提示: DeepSeek Harness 处于开发者预览阶段,预计会有破坏性兼容变更｡请固定你构建所依赖的版本,并在升级前关注 GitHub 仓库的发布说明｡ ｢一切皆插件｣的设计哲学 DeepSeek Harness 最重要的理念,在其官方文档中表述为:一切皆插件｡整个架构建立在 Cordis 插件系统之上,而 Cordis 正是为时空可组合性(spatiotemporal composability)而设计的——这一编程范式在 Cordis 论文《A Programming Paradigm for Spatiotemporal Composability》中有详细阐述｡ Cordis 扮演元框架的角色:它只负责插件的加载与卸载以及依赖关系管理｡DeepSeek Harness 的所有具体组件——模型适配器､工具注册表､会话日志､乃至 Agent 循环本身——都是不同的 Cordis 插件｡插件通过 Cordis 服务与类型化事件彼此协作,并可以在配置层自由组合｡ 这意味着开发者无需修改 DeepSeek Harness 的源码,就能独立选择､替换或扩展其中任一能力｡想换模型提供商?替换模型插件即可｡想换沙箱?替换沙箱插件｡想要完全不同的 Agent 循环?挂载你自己的插件｡DeepSeek Harness 没有需要打补丁的特权核心——你只需在其它插件旁边挂载一个新插件,注册效果会在插件卸载时干净地回滚｡ ✅ 最佳实践: 把 DeepSeek Harness 的每一项能力都视为可替换的｡在写自定义代码之前,先检查是否已有现成插件——这个生态系统的设计原则是｢组合优于修改｣｡ DeepSeek Harness 的四种运行模式 针对不同的使用场景,DeepSeek Harness 提供四种模式,每种模式默认加载不同的插件集合: 模式 加载内容 适用场景 标准模式 完整的工具组合 日常 Agent 工作 PTC 模式 程序化工具调用(Programmatic Tool Calling)——模型生成一段代码来组合多轮工具调用 复杂的多步骤工具工作流 极简模式 仅保留一个 shell 工具与一个文件编辑工具 最小环境下的模型基准测试 创造模式 检查当前运行时､在内存中试验 Cordis 插件,并据此组合和创作新的模式 构建与测试新的插件组合 PTC 模式尤其值得关注｡程序化工具调用是 DeepSeek Harness 的特色设计:模型不再逐次发起多个工具调用,而是生成一段代码,由这段代码编排多个工具调用的顺序｡这减少了往返次数､提升了可靠性,也让模型能以更富表现力的方式组合工具｡ 创造模式对插件开发者同样极具价值｡它允许你检查运行中的运行时､在内存中试验 Cordis 插件,并基于所学组合出新的模式——无需重启即可设计 DeepSeek Harness 的下一种配置｡ 追记式会话日志与 Trajectory 视图 DeepSeek Harness 最受开发者欢迎的特性之一是其可观测性｡每一次运行都有迹可循:模型看到的一切——系统提示词､思维链､工具调用与结果､子 Agent 调度､每一次上下文注入——都会写入追记式(append-only)会话日志｡ 在 Trajectory 视图中,你可以按来源查看这些信息｡由于日志是追记式的,任何内容都不会被静默改写,发生的事实被原样保留｡恢复､分叉､检索与回放共享同一份事件流:你可以从断点恢复会话､向新方向分叉､检索历史记录,或通过回放理解发生了什么｡ 这一设计带来了实际的调试收益｡当 Agent 运行出错时,DeepSeek Harness 的会话日志会精确展示模型在每一步看到了什么——确切的系统提示词､确切的工具输出､确切的上下文注入｡对于构建生产级 Agent 的团队而言,这种级别的可追溯性意味着｢知道｣而非｢猜测｣｡ 架构:Profile､Bundle 与核心包 在内部,运行中的 DeepSeek Harness 是一棵在启动时按有序层级组合而成的插件树｡两个概念组织这棵树:Profile 与 Bundle｡ Profile 是存储在 Harness 主目录中的命名组合｡它列出叠加的 Bundle､安装的外部插件,并保存用户自己的 cordis.patch.yml｡web 与 headless 两个 Profile 作为模板随附｡Bundle 是 Cordis 配置行及其挂载代码的分发格式,Bundle 插入的任何内容都可被上层补丁覆盖｡ 所有 Profile 的基础层是 dsh-base,提供模型适配器､工具､持久化､沙箱与审批策略､设置､凭证､遥测｡dsh-web-app 添加浏览器应用,dsh-headless 添加无服务器的单次运行器｡你可以用以下命令查看机器实际启动的插件树: dsh --profile web --dump-config 该命令输出的任何一行都可以用你自己的补丁替换｡DeepSeek Harness 的核心包与职责清晰对应: 包 职责 ctx 键 core/session 追记式 SessionEvent 日志与内存存储 ctx.sessions core/system-prompt 提示词分区与工具 schema 组装 ctx.systemPrompt core/tools 作用域工具注册表与受控执行管道 ctx.tools core/agent Agent 接口､实时注册表､agent/* 事件 ctx.agents core/agent-loop 实现该接口的默认驱动 ctx.agentLoop core/scope 每 Agent 作用域注册原语 库(无键) 如何开始使用 DeepSeek Harness 开始使用 DeepSeek Harness 只需几分钟｡最快的路径是 Web UI: npx @deepseek-ai/dsh web 该命令启动 Web UI,默认在 http://127.0.0.1:3080 提供服务｡之后的工作流非常简单: 配置模型:打开 Settings → Models,输入 DeepSeek API 密钥并保存｡无需重启服务器,模型路由立即可用｡模型配置指南还支持其他提供商与自定义 OpenAI 兼容端点｡ 选择工作区:点击 Choose workspace,添加启动 dsh 的项目目录并选中｡未选择工作区之前,会话编辑器不可用｡ 运行任务:启动会话并发送提示词｡Agent 可以读写工作区文件､执行命令､委派工作并维护计划｡Web UI 会在当前权限策略下需要审批的操作前征询确认｡ 如果希望从源码运行: git clone https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness cd deepseek-harness pnpm install pnpm run build pnpm dsh web dsh 进程以调用目录作为默认文件系统位置,全新的 Web UI 在添加工作区之前没有已选工作区｡对于无头自动化,DeepSeek Harness 还提供无服务器的单次运行器 Profile,以及用于程序化调用的 Python SDK｡ ⚠️ 注意: dsh 的 Web UI 会在当前权限策略下需要审批的操作前征询确认｡在运行不受信任的任务之前,请审慎配置权限策略｡ 为 DeepSeek Harness 开发插件 由于 DeepSeek Harness 中一切都是插件,插件开发就是主要的扩展路径｡架构文档建议在编写插件之前先阅读 Cordis 入门与教程,因为服务､类型化事件与可逆效果这一框架模型是整个系统的基础｡ DeepSeek Harness 的插件向共享上下文贡献服务､类型化事件与可逆效果｡插件卸载时注册随之回滚,不会留下孤立状态｡插件以 Bundle 形式分发,在 package.json 的 dsh 字段中声明:dsh.profile 列出 Profile 的 Bundle,dsh.bundle 指向 Bundle 的补丁文件｡ 插件作者的实际工作流是:先在创造模式中实验,检查运行时并在内存中测试 Cordis 插件;然后将插件打包为 Bundle;最后通过编辑 cordis.patch.yml 或传入 --patch 覆盖层将其挂载到 Profile｡--dump-config 命令会显示机器启动时的确切配置行,让你始终清楚该补丁什么｡ DeepSeek Harness 团队明确欢迎插件生态:公告邀请全球 Harness 开发者共同在开放､可复用､可组合的基础设施之上建设 DSH 插件生态｡如果你开发了插件,可以为仓库添加 dsh-plugin 主题标签以提高可发现性｡ DeepSeek Harness 与其他 Agent Harness 对比 DeepSeek Harness 与开发者们熟悉的 Agent Harness 有何不同?差异主要在架构层面: 维度 DeepSeek Harness 典型 Harness 架构 一切皆插件(Cordis) 单体核心 + 扩展 扩展路径 挂载插件,无需改源码 Fork 或补丁核心 可观测性 追记式会话日志 + Trajectory 视图 各不相同,往往不完整 运行模式 标准 / PTC / 极简 / 创造 通常只有一种 许可证 MIT,开源 各不相同 工具调用 经典 + PTC(代码组合调用) 通常只有经典方式 最关键的差异在于插件接缝｡DeepSeek Harness 没有需要打补丁的特权核心——包括 Agent 循环本身在内的所有组件都可以从配置层替换｡对于已经触到某个 Harness 扩展点上限的团队来说,这是实质性的区别｡追记式会话日志也领先于多数替代方案——后者通常只记录工具调用,而不记录每一次上下文注入｡ 常见问题解答 Q: DeepSeek Harness 是免费开源的么? A: 是的｡DeepSeek Harness 以 MIT 协议开源,完整源码位于 github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness｡v0.1 开发者预览版已于 2026 年 8 月 13 日向全球社区开放｡ Q: 如何快速启动 DeepSeek Harness? A: 在装有 Node.js 的终端中执行 npx @deepseek-ai/dsh web,Web UI 将在 http://127.0.0.1:3080 启动｡随后在 Settings → Models 中添加模型 API 密钥并选择工作区即可｡ Q: ｢一切皆插件｣在实践中意味着什么? A: 意味着 DeepSeek Harness 的所有能力——模型､工具､技能､会话､沙箱､存储､循环､调度､UI——都是 Cordis 插件｡你可以在不修改 DeepSeek Harness 自身源码的情况下选择､替换或扩展其中任意一项｡ Q: DeepSeek Harness 的四种模式是什么? A: 标准模式(完整工具集)､PTC 模式(模型编写代码组合工具调用的程序化工具调用)､极简模式(仅 shell",

Harness的插件生态在GitHub上发展,以“dsh-plugin”为标签的公开仓库数量超过700个,涵盖了从生产力工具到娱乐游戏的各类插件 [4]｡ 主要功能 DeepSeek Harness就是给DeepSeek装上了“手”和“脚”,让它能自己去改代码､查资料､运行测试,甚至完成一整套复杂任务,直到把活干完提交｡DeepSeek Harness具有项目管理､长任务协作､多智能体编排､上下文管理､联网搜索､调用外部技能等功能｡ [1] DeepSeek Harness有一个独特的设计理念:一切皆插件｡无论是底层的模型､调用的工具,还是数据存储和交互界面,所有模块都可以自由替换和重组｡可以用DeepSeek自己的模型,也可以接入Kimi､OpenAI､谷歌等近数十家厂商的大模型;内置了“极简模式”应付日常问答,“代码模式”处理批量搜索和自动化任务;想怎么组合,全凭需求 [1]｡ 内测评价 2026年8月,一位参与内测的网友在海外社交平台上感叹:“内测2天之后,愿称DeepSeek Harness为宇宙最强｡”也有用户表示“初步给到夯,可能将直接冲击现有的AI编程工具市场”,还有用户将它和DeepSeek的鲸鱼logo联系起来,调侃称“现在终于是完整体鲸鱼侠了 [1]”｡ 插件生态 DeepSeek Harness于2026年8月14日发布开发者预览版(v0.1)并开源,其核心设计思路是“一切皆插件”,底层基于Cordis插件系统,提供标准､极简､创造､PTC四种模式｡发布后社区反响热烈,半小时内GitHub星数破万,截至发稿时已超过10万｡ [6] 发布不到24小时,GitHub上已有288个插件仓库,社区内测生态已建设一个多月｡ [8] DeepSeek Harness以“万物皆插件(Everything is a Plugin)”为设计理念｡截至2026年8月,在GitHub上以“dsh-plugin”为标签的公开仓库已超过700个｡ [4] 这些插件旨在增强核心功能与工作效率｡例如dsh-agent-teams支持在会话中快速组建和管理多智能体协作团队｡DSH Better Sidebar将文件管理､代码编辑､终端､Git面板等功能集成到侧边栏｡dsh-at-file提供类似@文件的指令功能｡dsh-memory-evolve为智能体提供跨会话的长期记忆能力｡此外还有迁移与集成工具如dsh-plugin-claude-bridge和dsh-claude-move｡ [4] 这类插件展现了社区的丰富想象力｡包括为界面添加电子宠物､集成包含俄罗斯方块､贪吃蛇等在内的18款小游戏合集(dsh-minigames),以及模拟2005年代中文互联网风格的广告UI(dsh-ads)等｡ [4] 插件类型极其多样,包括功能增强类(如双模型路由dsh-plan-execute､视觉能力扩展dsh-vision)､界面主题类(如复古QQ皮肤dsh-qq2006､像素鲸鱼dsh-ui-whale)､娱乐游戏类(如五子棋dsh-gomoku､摸鱼面板dsh-lazyfish),甚至出现了插件间的攻防(广告插件dsh-ads与去广告插件dsh-anti-ads)｡ [8] 社区将DSH生态类比为《我的世界》:DSH本体是原版生存模式,插件是Mod,awesome-dsh-plugins等目录站是CurseForge,dsh-mega等整合包对应MC整合包｡有开发者制作了名为“fabric”的插件,其简介明确指向《我的世界》的Mod加载器,印证了这一类比｡ [8] 此外,社区开发者还基于官方开源项目构建了DeepSeek Harness Desktop桌面应用｡该应用将原本需要通过命令行启动的Harness本地Web UI封装为独立应用,用户无需安装Node.js或执行命令,即可直接在macOS､Windows上使用｡ [6] 为了方便用户发现和使用插件,社区开发者维护了多个“Awesome DeepSeek Harness Plugins”精选列表｡ [4] API与定价 DeepSeek于2026年8月14日宣布对其API进行调价,采用峰谷定价模式,并于2026年8月17日生效,峰值价格最高涨至27元｡官方称“智效比”故事进入下半场,模型价格便宜但有时段性差异 [6]｡ 技术架构与使用 DeepSeek Harness(命令行名)是一个开源的智能体(Agent)运行时,其官方定义为“Model + Harness = Agent”｡它不是模型,也不是聊天界面,而是将模型､工具､工作区､权限､会话记忆和任务循环串联起来的中间层｡其核心设计理念是“一切皆插件”,底层基于Cordis框架构建,该内核源自Koishi聊天机器人框架,具备“可逆副作用”特性,能追踪并自动回收插件注册时产生的副作用,从而实现真正的热插拔(安装/卸载插件无需重启)｡模型适配器､工具注册表､会话日志乃至Agent循环本身均为可替换的插件 [6-7]｡Harness内置了四种工作模式,以适应不同场景:标准模式､极简模式､创造模式以及PTC(Programmatic Tool Calling)模式｡PTC模式允许模型编写程序一次性执行长流程任务,中间数据保留在执行环境中,可显著节省Token消耗 [7]｡ Harness提供了强大的可观测性功能,包括会话日志与轨迹视图｡用户可以通过轨迹视图回溯Agent执行任务的每一步,Trajectory将模型看到的所有信息(系统提示词､推理过程､工具调用及结果､子agent调度､每一次上下文注入)记录在一份只增不改的会话日志中,提供完整的可观测性,便于问题复盘和会话恢复､分叉｡包括工具调用､命令行执行､模型的思考链(Chain-of-Thought)以及详细的耗时､Token消耗等信息 [7]｡ 用户可通过Node.js环境使用一行命令快速启动Harness｡启动后,通过Web界面进行配置,首要步骤是配置API密钥与模型｡Harness默认优先支持DeepSeek模型(如DeepSeek-V4-Pro),但也内置了对Anthropic､OpenAI等主流模型提供商的支持,并允许用户添加自定义模型端点,体现了其模型无关性 [7]｡其插件生态已快速涌现大量应用,例如提供不同隔离方案的沙箱插件(如sandbox-micro),以及可将Harness接入QQ､微信､飞书等平台的“远程渠道”插件(如qqbot､dsh-weixin-bot),使其能作为聊天机器人运行 [4]｡配置完成后,用户需选择本地工作区,此后便可在会话中向Agent分派任务,Agent可在授权范围内读写文件､执行命令､派发子任务等 [7]｡ 除交互式Web界面外,Harness支持Headless(无头)模式,通过命令执行一次性任务后自动退出,适用于脚本化批量任务或CI/CD流水线｡此外,项目还提供了Python SDK､JSON-RPC SDK及ACP服务端,便于开发者将Harness的能力嵌入到自有应用程序中 [7]｡ 版本与开发状态 DeepSeek Harness(命令行名dsh)于2026年8月13日晚20:30正式开源其开发者预览版｡发布后,其GitHub仓库Star数迅速突破5万｡官方在README中明确警告存在不兼容的变更,会话格式不提供兼容承诺,升级后旧的会话记录可能无法读取｡其警告措辞以全大写字母强调:“THERE WILL BE COMPATIBILITY-BREAKING CHANGES”(一定会有破坏兼容性的变更)｡因此,官方表示当前版本适合评估和内部试验,不适合作为唯一的生产工具｡关于社区贡献,主仓库暂时不接收外部PR,官方建议在GitHub Discussions反馈问题或自行开发插件,并明确表示主仓库里的包并不比社区的包更重要 [7]｡ 竞争策略与影响 DeepSeek Harness与Claude Code存在根本区别,Claude Code是闭源､高度集成的产品,其模型､工具和调度策略由Anthropic决定并紧密耦合,构成商业壁垒;而DSH从发布起就开源,采用插件化架构,将智能体外壳模块化｡DeepSeek通过开源DSH,意图将harness层的价值拉平为公共品,从而将竞争压回模型本身的能力和价格维度,这有利于发挥其在模型领域的优势,其策略类似R1开源权重冲击OpenAI o1溢价 [8]｡ DSH的定位并非Claude Code平替,而是成为类似Eclipse的平台或《我的世界》的地基,其官方模式只是其上的“官方发行版”之一｡其社区生态展现出包容性与侵略性,已出现大量兼容或替代竞品的插件,如dsh-cc-tui(模仿Claude Code界面)､dsh-opencode-server(实现OpenCode API)､official-plugins-port(移植Claude Code/Codex官方插件)以及dsh-session-hub(导入竞品会话历史)等 [8]｡ DSH的开源策略可能颠覆闭源智能体外壳的商业模式,但也面临挑战 [8]｡官方在README中明确警告存在不兼容的变更(THERE WILL BE COMPATIBILITY-BREAKING CHANGES),会话格式不提供兼容承诺,升级后旧的会话记录可能无法读取 [7]｡同时,一个人人可以往里挂插件､插件能接触shell和文件系统的架构,也带来了潜在的安全风险 [8]｡ 早期反馈与风险 产品发布后,社区反馈呈现两极分化｡一方面,其高度开放的插件化架构和类似《我的世界》的生态模式被赞扬为具有巨大潜力和创造性;另一方面,也开始出现批评其“故弄玄虚”､设计过于复杂的声音 [8]｡ 其开放式插件架构带来了显著的安全隐患｡由于插件具备访问本地Shell和文件系统的能力,攻击面巨大｡在社区组织dsh-external中,已存在名为“dsh-security”的仓库,其简介为“DSH现有的可行的攻击链demo”,主动展示了潜在的安全威胁 [8]｡ 参考资料 1今晚正式发布!DeepSeek终于不只是会“聊天”了.都市时报 [引用日期2026-08-14] 2DeepSeek V4       "snippet": "打开网易新闻 查看精彩图片 智东西作者 陈骏达编辑 心缘 智东西4月30日报道,今天,DeepSeek发布多模态技术报告《用视觉原语思考(Thinking with Visaul Primitives)》,详细阐释了昨日灰度上线的DeepSeek识图模式背后的技术细节(DeepSeek终于能看图了 Produzioni dal Basso｡其中的创新点在于强化学习阶段的奖励模型｡ 以迷宫任务为例,奖励分解为探索进度､撞墙惩罚､路径有效性和探索完整性等多个维度｡模型每正确探索一个单元格､没有非法穿越墙壁,都会获得正向信号,而一旦发生撞墙,即便最终的答案为“可解”,也会被严格扣分｡ 这种稠密的奖励机制,让模型必须认真对待每一个视觉原语操作,无法靠猜答案实现奖励破解｡ 为了同时掌握框定位和点指向这两种视觉原语,该团队还分别训练了两个专家模型,最后通过在线策略蒸馏将它们融合成一个统一模型,让学生模型在自己生成的思维轨迹上,学习两位专家老师的输出分布｡这种设计有效避免了两种异构原语在训练中的相互干扰｡ 值得一提的是,这项工作的技术路线建立在一个高效的视觉编码架构之上｡ 首先,Vision Transformer以14×14的块大小将图像切分成视觉token;然后,在ViT输出端进行3×3的空间压缩,将每9个相邻token沿通道维度合并为1个;最后,利用模型底座DeepSeek-V4-Flash自带的压缩稀疏注意力机制,将KV缓存中的视觉条目再压缩4倍｡ 以一张756×756分辨率的图像为例,它原本会产生2916个patch token,经过三级压缩后最终仅保留81个视觉KV条目,整体压缩比高达7056倍｡ 这种token效率意味着,模型在展开复杂的空间推理时拥有了一份“提炼好的索引”,可以拿着索引直接进行思考,从工程上就减弱了无关像素对推理链路的干扰｡ 结语:多模态智能的“系统二”进化 DeepSeek多模态团队也在报告中提到了当前技术的边界｡模型在复杂拓扑推理任务上的跨场景泛化能力尚未完善,且思考中视觉基元的激活目前仍依赖显式的触发词,尚未实现完全的自发调用｡ 但他们也认为,这套框架为多模态社区展示了通往系统二级别的多模态智能的路径｡这一路径没有一味地堆高图像分辨率,而在构建了更精准从参照指标｡ 用空间坐标锚定抽象思维,让模型像人类一样“边指边想”,这本身就是一个值得继续深挖的方向｡",
      "date": ""
    },
    {
      "title": "边指边想!DeepSeek 多模态核心技术全公开,视觉推理再突破",
      "link": "http://deepseek.club/topic/1769",
      "snippet": "边指边想!DeepSeek 多模态核心技术全公开,视觉推理再突破 DeepSeek.club｡2026 年 4 月 30 日,DeepSeek 正式发布多模态技术报告《Thinking with Visual Primitives(用视觉原语思考)》,完整揭秘此前灰度上线的识图能力背后的核心技术逻辑｡这项突破让大模型告别纯语言思维链,实现 “语言逻辑 + 空间坐标” 双轨思考,在多模态领域迈出关键一步｡ 本次上线的识图模式,搭载284B 参数､13B 激活的多模态推理模型,基座为 DeepSeek-V4-Flash,后续模型权重将整合至基础模型并正式开放｡ 一､破解指代难题:从 “看清” 到 “精准指向” 当前主流多模态大模型,大多聚焦弥补 “感知鸿沟”,通过提升分辨率､精细化分块让模型看得更清｡但 DeepSeek 团队发现,即便感知拉满,复杂视觉推理仍易失效｡ 核心痛点在于自然语言指代鸿沟:面对密集场景,“左边的物体”“上方的区域” 等描述无法让模型精确定位,多步推理极易因指代模糊逻辑崩塌｡ 为此,团队提出视觉原语思路 —— 让模型像人一样 “边想边指”,用坐标点､边界框作为思维最小单元,在推理中精准锚定视觉目标,从根源解决定位偏差问题｡ 架构上,模型由 DeepSeek-ViT 负责图像特征提取,文本分词器处理指令输入,两者在 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 基座融合推理,最终输出 “自然语言 + 视觉原语” 的联合结果,兼顾文本理解与原生视觉定位｡ 二､千万级数据训练:让模型学会 “精准指向” 要让视觉原语融入思维链,核心是让模型把 “指向” 内化为思考习惯｡DeepSeek 构建了预训练 - 冷启动 - 强化学习全流程训练体系｡ 预训练阶段 从互联网挖掘近 9.8 万个目标检测相关数据源,经自动化质量筛查,剔除乱码､超大框､低精度标注等无效数据,最终筛选出超4000 万个高质量精准样本,夯实模型基础定位能力｡ 冷启动阶段 针对计数､空间推理､迷宫导航､路径追踪四大核心任务,合成带精确思考轨迹的监督数据｡例如计数任务先批量框选目标再累加,迷宫任务用坐标标记每一步位置,强制模型建立 “指向 - 推理” 强关联｡ 三､稠密奖励 + 极致压缩:效率与精度双升 完成基础训练后,团队通过 “训练专家再融合” 策略精细化能力,创新点集中在强化学习奖励机制与视觉编码压缩｡",
      "date": "2026/05/05 00:00:00"
    },
    {
      "title": "DeepSeek又过节发论文:7056倍视觉压缩,解决多模态模型“找不准”痼疾",
      "link": "https://new.qq.com/rain/a/20260501A02MSR00?refer=cp_1009",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek又过节发论文:7056倍视觉压缩,解决多模态模型“找不准”痼疾｡刚刚开放“识图”功能灰度测试的 DeepSeek ,在昨天同步发了篇多模态的论文｡DeepSeek\"睁眼\"了!实测其识图能力够用但不惊艳论文题目为“Thinking with Visual Primitives”(以视觉原语思考),提出了一种新的推理范式“视觉原语”｡论文指出,当前主流的多模态大模型普遍存在“感知鸿沟”(Perception Gap)的问题｡模型能看清图片,但推理时只能用自然语言描述“左边那个大的”､“靠近中央的红色物体”等｡普遍存在细粒度理解失效､指代混淆､空间关系判错､密集小目标无法区分等核心问题｡在面对密集场景时,这种模糊的语言描述,必然导致注意力的“漂移”与推理的崩塌｡此前,业界主流解法是,提高分辨率让模型看更清,即堆参数量､加大编码器､扩充训练数据｡但这种只用网格Patch特征做图文对齐的方式,表征无显式坐标､无独立寻址能力､不可拆解推理,只能做全局模糊语义匹配,成了高精度视觉推理长期遇瓶颈的本质原因｡DeepSeek 换了思路,它认为,问题不在看,“在指”｡论文提出的解决方案是,把关键点､坐标点､包围框､语义掩码､空间线段作为模型原生推理原子｡模型每提到一个视觉对象,同步输出坐标,像锚点一样把逻辑链钉在图片的物理位置上｡边界框用于需要定位和尺寸的对象,点坐标用于迷宫轨迹和曲线追踪｡这套架构有效性的另一大支柱在于,极致的效率｡模型基于DeepSeek V4-Flash(一个284B总参数､推理时激活13B的MoE模型)构建｡一张756×756的图片经ViT处理后会产生2916个图像块token,再通过3×3空间压缩和其自研的压缩稀疏注意力机制(CSA)进行深度压缩,最终只需81个视觉KV条目即可支持复杂的空间推理｡作为对比,同等条件下,Claude Sonnet 4.6大约需要870个token,而Gemini-3-Flash则约需1100个｡这意味着,思考过程的每一步都前所未有地“轻量”,模型无需在记忆的汪洋中反复检索被稀释的视觉信息,实现了7056倍的视觉压缩｡模型的训练和表现也验证了这一框架的威力｡团队从近10万个数据源中筛选出约3.17万个高质量目标检测数据集,生成了超过4000万条训练样本,覆盖计数､空间推理､迷宫导航和路径追踪四类核心任务｡在后训练阶段,他们采用了颇具匠心的“先专家化､后统一”策略:先分别训练擅长边界框和点坐标的两个专家模型,再通过在线策略蒸馏将二者合并｡",
      "date": "2026/05/01 09:23:22"
    },
    {
      "title": "Thinking with Visaul Primitives",
      "link": "https://baike.baidu.com/item/Thinking with Visaul Primitives/67726384",
      "snippet": "Thinking with Visaul Primitives 百度百科｡订阅更新 0 有用+1 0 Thinking with Visaul Primitives 播报 编辑 讨论 上传视频 DeepSeek多模态团队发布的技术论文 《Thinking with Visaul Primitives》是DeepSeek多模态团队于2026年4月30日发布的一篇多模态模型技术论文 [1] ｡该论文针对多模态大模型中存在的“指代鸿沟”(Reference Gap)问题,提出了将点､边界框等空间标记作为“思维的最小单元”直接嵌入模型推理轨迹的核心框架,旨在实现语言逻辑与空间坐标交织的双轨思维 ｡论文还介绍了其基于 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 语言主干构建的 混合专家模型 ( MoE ),以及通过图像分块编码与 压缩稀疏注意力 等技术实现高达7056倍的视觉信息压缩 [2-3] ｡ 论文发布后, DeepSeek 官方连夜删除了多模态仓库和论文原文 [1] ｡随后在2026年5月,DeepSeek开始大范围灰度测试并上线了基于该技术框架的“识图模式” [7] ｡ 外文名 Thinking with Visaul Primitives 发表日期 2026年4月30日 相关视频 查看全部 目录 1 内容简介 2 技术背景与问题 3 核心创新 4 模型架构与训练 5 实验结果 6 局限与未来 7 相关事件 8 技术应用与反响 内容简介 播报 编辑 论文指出,目前的多模态模型在复杂任务上崩溃,不是因为看不见(感知鸿沟),而在于“指不准”(引用鸿沟)｡ 自然语言天然具有模糊性｡当用户要求模型处理复杂的空间布局时,仅靠文字描述很容易产生歧义｡就像数一堆散落的硬币,如果不用手指逐个按着确认,人类也很容易数错或数重｡ DeepSeek的解法是给模型配上一只“手指”｡通过引入“视觉原语”(Visual Primitives)框架,模型将点､边界框等空间标记提升为最小思维单元｡这意味着模型在推理时,能够一边“想”一边“指”,将抽象的语言逻辑指向到具体空间坐标｡ 这一灵感借鉴了人类的认知｡论文提及,人类走迷宫或数密集物体时,会用手指这类指示性指向来降低认知负荷､维持逻辑一致性｡通过将视觉原语嵌入思考,模型可以模拟人类这种“指向-推理”的协同｡ 这一模型基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash(总参数2840亿)构建｡",
      "date": "2026/05/09 09:57:39"
    },
    {
      "title": "DeepSeek Unveils Open‑Source Multimodal Model: “Thinking with Visual Primitives”",
      "link": "https://www.besthub.dev/articles/deepseek-unveils-open-source-multimodal-model-thinking-with-visual-primitives-9eeb47accef9",
      "snippet": "DeepSeek Unveils Open‑Source Multimodal Model: “Thinking with Visual Primitives” BestHub｡DeepSeek releases an open‑source multimodal LLM that introduces a visual‑primitive framework—elevating bounding boxes and points to token level—to close the reference gap, achieve extreme KV‑cache compression, and outperform GPT‑5.4, Claude‑Sonnet‑4.6 and Gemini‑3‑Flash on counting, spatial reasoning, maze navigation and path‑tracing benchmarks. 1. Problem Redefinition: From Perception Gap to Reference Gap Current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) perform Chain‑of‑Thought reasoning almost entirely in language space. Even when cutting‑edge models solve the Perception Gap with high‑resolution cropping or dynamic tiling, they still suffer logical collapse on dense counting, topological navigation and multi‑step spatial reasoning. The authors identify a deeper bottleneck called the Reference Gap: natural language is fuzzy and continuous, while visual space is precise and discrete. Describing an object such as “the second red object on the left” discards the exact spatial anchor, causing the reasoning chain to drift from the image and produce cascading hallucinations. Natural language is inherently vague, whereas visual space is exact; describing an object with words discards precise spatial anchors, leading to hallucinations. Humans resolve this by pointing with a finger, anchoring abstract semantics to concrete coordinates and reducing working‑memory load. The paper proposes “Thinking with Visual Primitives”: elevate bounding boxes and points to the same level as language tokens and interleave them directly into the model’s reasoning trace, enabling the model to “point while it reasons”. 2. Architecture and Training Pipeline: Balancing Efficiency and Specialized Capability 2.1 Architecture Design The model follows a LLaVA‑style architecture. DeepSeek‑V4‑Flash (284 B total parameters, 13 B MoE activation parameters) serves as the language backbone, while the visual encoder is the proprietary DeepSeek‑ViT , which supports arbitrary‑resolution inputs. Key compression techniques: 14×14 Patch Embedding : split the image into basic patches. 3×3 Spatial Compression : compress nine adjacent patches into one token along the channel dimension. Compressed Sparse Attention (CSA) : further compress visual tokens in the LLM KV‑Cache layer. Example: a 756×756 image (571 536 pixels) → ViT produces 2 916 patch tokens → 3×3 compression yields 324 tokens → CSA leaves only 81 visual KV entries, an overall compression ratio of 7 056 : 1. Architecture diagram 2.2 Five‑Stage Post‑Training Process Pretraining : pre‑train on tens of trillions of multimodal tokens to give the model basic visual‑primitive generation ability. Specialized SFT : build cold‑start datasets for Box (FTwG) and Point (FTwP) tasks, fine‑tune them separately to avoid modality conflict. Specialized RL : apply GRPO reinforcement learning to each expert model with three reward components—format, quality, accuracy. Unified RFT : generate rejection‑sampling data from the two experts and train a single fused model. On‑Policy Distillation : distill the expert output distributions into the unified model via reverse KL. 3. Cold‑Start Data Construction: Fine‑Grained Design of Four Reasoning Scenarios 3.1 Counting MLLMs fail at dense counting because they cannot map language numbers to visual entities one‑to‑one. Coarse‑grained counting : the model first parses intent, then performs batch grounding (boxes all candidates) and finally sums the detections. In a team‑photo example the model boxes 25 people at once and verifies the count. Fine‑grained counting : based on GQA scene graphs, construct attribute‑constrained queries (e.g., “how many bears are on the ground?”). The model enumerates each candidate and discards those violating the attribute. 3.2 Spatial Reasoning & General VQA Using GQA and CLEVR, the model must perform multi‑hop logical reasoning such as “does a purple rubber object of the same size as the gray metal sphere exist?”. Each step is anchored with a token sequence ...... that ties the mentioned object to image coordinates, preventing semantic drift. 3.3 Maze Navigation To test topological reasoning, the authors generate rectangular, circular, and hexagonal mazes with DFS, Prim and Kruskal algorithms, inserting deliberately unsolvable sections. The model records each step with [[x,y]], producing a human‑like trial‑and‑error DFS trace. Maze navigation example 3.4 Path Tracing In tangled Bézier curves the model must follow a specified line to its endpoint. The challenge is disambiguating intersections;",
      "date": "2026/04/30 00:00:00"
    },
    {
      "title": "How DeepSeek’s New Paper Redefines Multimodal Reasoning with Visual Primitives",
      "link": "https://www.besthub.dev/articles/how-deepseek-s-new-paper-redefines-multimodal-reasoning-with-visual-primitives-50bed3688bfc",
      "snippet": "How DeepSeek’s New Paper Redefines Multimodal Reasoning with Visual Primitives BestHub｡DeepSeek’s new paper \"Thinking with Visual Primitives\" tackles the reference gap in multimodal models by introducing points and boxes as reasoning units, achieving up to 8× token efficiency and leading benchmark scores in counting, spatial reasoning, and maze navigation compared with GPT‑5.4, Claude‑Sonnet‑4.6 and Gemini‑3‑Flash. DeepSeek’s paper \"Thinking with Visual Primitives\" identifies a critical \"Reference Gap\" in multimodal large language models: while they can perceive objects, they cannot consistently refer to them during reasoning. The authors propose elevating spatial markers—points and bounding boxes—to the smallest reasoning units, interleaving them with text tokens in a chain‑of‑thought. Example token syntax is shown as [[309,512]] or [[x1,y1,x2,y2]], anchoring logic to physical coordinates. Architecture-wise, the model builds on DeepSeek‑V4‑Flash (284 B total parameters, 13 B MoE) and a custom DeepSeek‑ViT. Visual engineering details include a 3×3 spatial compression that merges nine adjacent patch tokens, Compressed Sparse Attention (CSA) that further reduces KV cache size by fourfold, and an overall compression ratio of 7 056× (a 756×756 image is represented by only 81 KV entries). The training pipeline consists of four stages: expert SFT, specialized RL, unified RFT, and on‑policy distillation. Instead of using small datasets like COCO, the team collected massive web‑scale bounding‑box data because bbox annotations are deterministic, can generalize to points, and carry richer positional information. The RL stage for maze navigation employs detailed rewards (coverage, exploration completeness, wall‑accuracy, final path validity) that mimic a human solving process: mark start/end, explore, backtrack, and output the full path. Benchmark results on 11 tasks show DeepSeek matching or surpassing GPT‑5.4, Claude‑Sonnet‑4.6, and Gemini‑3‑Flash while using only about one‑eighth of their visual token budget. Notable scores include CountQA EM/RA@10 = 64.9/74.1 (second only to Gemini‑3‑Flash), Pixmo‑Count = 89.2 (top), DS_Spatial_Reasoning = 98.7 (large margin), DS_Maze_Navigation = 66.9 (second vs 50.6), and DS_Path_Tracing = 56.7 (second vs 46.5). These results demonstrate that pure language chain‑of‑thought struggles with topological reasoning without visual primitives. Token efficiency vs average score comparison Case studies illustrate the approach: Counting Pikachu – the model draws a box around each Pokemon and correctly outputs the count 6. Chinese world‑knowledge query – despite no Chinese visual‑primitive data, the model locates the Golden Gate Bridge, recognizes San Francisco, and answers that the local NBA team is the Warriors. Maze navigation – using to mark start (green diamond) and goal (red tag), the model iteratively explores, backtracks at dead ends, and finally outputs a complete feasible path. Fine-grained counting example Chinese world knowledge reasoning example Maze navigation example Key observations from the author: DeepSeek continues its \"compression philosophy\"—doing more with fewer tokens—across OCR and now visual‑primitive reasoning. The Reference Gap is a more fundamental bottleneck than the Perception Gap; mainstream multimodal models collapse on dense counting, maze, and complex scene understanding because language cannot precisely refer to visual space. Limitations include dependence on input resolution (fine‑grained scenes still suffer bias), reliance on explicit trigger words to activate the mechanism, and imperfect cross‑scene generalization of point‑based reasoning. For developers, the approach is promising for applications such as complex chart/UI interpretation, dense object counting in retail or warehouse settings, topological reasoning on schematics, maps, or circuit diagrams, and robot path‑planning VLMs. If the DeepSeek‑V4‑VL model becomes open‑source, these scenarios could see a noticeable accuracy boost. In summary, the paper’s greatest contribution is pinpointing the next hurdle for multimodal AI—shifting from \"seeing clearly\" to \"thinking clearly\" by giving models a pointing ability—realized through extreme token efficiency, visual‑primitive chain‑of‑thought, and a specialized expert‑training pipeline. Republication Notice This article has been distilled and summarized from source material, then republished for learning and reference. If you believe it infringes your rights, please contactadmin@besthub.devand we will review it promptly.",
      "date": "2026/05/04 00:00:00"
    },
    {
      "title": "The Vision Model That Wasn't There: DeepSeek V4, a Vanished Paper, and a Recipe You Can Use Today",
      "link": "https://www.jocheojeda.com/2026/06/24/deepseek-v4-vision-thinking-with-visual-primitives/",
      "snippet": "The Vision Model That Wasn't There: DeepSeek V4, a Vanished Paper, and a Recipe You Can Use Today Joche Ojeda｡ Because it's too specific to be a hallucination. \"Bounding boxes and relative dimensions, step by step\" is not the kind of thing a marketing bot invents from nothing. The plot twist: the paper that was published, then pulled It turns out DeepSeek absolutely did this work. It lives in a research paper called \"Thinking with Visual Primitives\" — and the reason you may not have heard of it is that it was published, cited, and then quietly pulled. The original repository 404s; what survives is an archived community mirror with the technical report and an MIT license, but no model weights. The idea is genuinely lovely, and worth knowing even if you can't run it. Most multimodal models reason in pure language — they \"see\" an image, then think about it in words. The paper's argument is that language is a lossy pointer: in a dense scene, the phrase \"the cup behind the other cup\" collapses, and the model loses track of which object it meant. They call this the Reference Gap. Their fix: make spatial markers — points and bounding boxes — into \"minimal units of thought,\" and interleave them directly into the reasoning trace. The model literally points while it reasons. The boxes aren't a final answer formatted at the end; they're scaffolding the model leans on mid-thought to keep itself anchored to physical coordinates. A few details I found striking: It's built on the DeepSeek-V4-Flash backbone (a 284B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts, ~13B active) plus an in-house vision encoder. So the marketing copy that confused me wasn't wrong about the lineage — it was describing the research model, not the text model that ships under the same name. The efficiency is the headline. A 756×756 image becomes ~324 visual tokens, and a compressed-attention trick squeezes the KV cache down to roughly 81 entries — an overall compression on the order of 7,000×. That's why it can think hard about an image without a giant token bill. On counting, spatial-reasoning, and topological (maze / path-tracing) benchmarks, it reportedly matches or beats GPT-5.4, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, and Gemini-3-Flash — at a fraction of the visual-token budget. And the output format, which is the part you can actually steal: cordless drill[[x1,y1,x2,y2]] Boxes are [x1,y1,x2,y2] — top-left and bottom-right — normalized to integers 0–999. Points use [[x,y]]. The reasoning follows a fixed protocol: intent analysis → batch grounding (locate everything at once) → summation, with an explicit \"faithful refusal\" rule so the model says \"not present\" instead of inventing a box for something it can't see. The lesson, before the code Here's the thing I want to underline, because it's the reusable takeaway and it cost me a few hours to internalize: A capability in a paper is not a capability in an API. A model that exists as weights-to-be-released-later is, for engineering purposes, a model that does not exist. But a method is portable even when the model isn't. The weights are gone (for now — \"to be integrated into the foundation model in the future\"). The 284B MoE would be a serious self-hosting project even if they weren't. But the recipe — the box format, the point-while-you-reason protocol, faithful refusal — is just a way of prompting. And that I can run today, on a model that can actually see. Porting the recipe to Gemini So I pointed the exact same protocol at Gemini 2.5 Flash (with Pro as the heavyweight option), in a small standalone sandbox, and measured it on real photos. One gotcha worth saving you: Gemini's native bounding-box format is [ymin, xmin, ymax, xmax] on a 0–1000 scale — the opposite axis order from the paper's [x1,y1,x2,y2]. If you don't pin the convention explicitly in the prompt, you'll get boxes that look plausible and are silently transposed. Say which one you want, every time. What I found, testing on natural photos: Identification is strong. On the classic two-cats-and-two-remotes test image it returned exactly that — two cats, two remotes — with usable color/category attributes. Faithful refusal works. I asked it to find a dog and a bicycle that weren't in the frame;",

The paper picked up traction May 4, then the PDF quietly disappeared from DeepSeek's site without explanation. Clones survive on GitHub. No retraction notice, no \"we'll be back,\" no statement. The technical content is the more interesting half of the story; the pull is the second half. The architectural pivot Multimodal models since GPT-4V have all done the same shape: the image is encoded into vision tokens, the language model reasons about those tokens in natural language, and the reasoning chain references the image through descriptions (\"the leftmost shape,\" \"the object behind the table\"). This is what DeepSeek calls the reference gap: language-mediated reference loses object identity across multi-step reasoning, because each new mention has to re-anchor against the image through a fuzzy linguistic predicate. DeepSeek's move is to let the model emit visual primitives — points and bounding boxes — directly into the chain-of-thought as inline tokens. The format is: labelx1,y1,x2,y2 The reasoning chain stays anchored in the image's coordinate system across every step. Step 4 of a maze trace can reference the box opened in step 1 by coordinate rather than by \"the corner from earlier.\" The reference is geometric, not linguistic. Built on V4-Flash (284B MoE, 13B active). Custom DeepSeek Vision Transformer with arbitrary-resolution 14×14 patches. The compression pipeline is the operational substrate: 756×756 image → 2,916 patch tokens → 324 tokens via 3×3 spatial compression → 81 KV cache entries via sparse attention ≈ 7,000× compression from raw pixels That compression is what makes interleaving coordinate primitives per-step affordable. If each visual reference cost the original 2,916 tokens, the chain wouldn't fit. The architecture and the reasoning mechanism co-evolved. Training was five stages: multimodal pre-training → two specialist models (grounding for boxes, pointing for coordinates) → GRPO on each with three reward heads (format, quality, accuracy) → unified RFT merging the specialists → on-policy distillation into one student model. The two-specialist-then-merge structure is interesting on its own — it's a different theory than the dominant single-model-with-multitask-RL approach. What the benchmark numbers actually say DeepSeek published one cherry-picked dimension (maze navigation) and explicitly footnoted that the scores \"cover only a subset of evaluation dimensions and are therefore not indicative of the model's overall capabilities.\" So the numbers are intentionally narrow: Maze navigation: 67% (vs. Gemini 3 Flash 49%, GPT-5.4 50%, Claude Sonnet 4.6 49%) The disclaimer matters because the gap (~17 absolute points) is exactly the kind of thing that screams regime shift on a single task — and exactly the kind of thing that often doesn't generalize. The honest read is: on tasks where the reference gap is the dominant cost (multi-step spatial reasoning with persistent object identity), coordinate-anchored CoT closes the gap to specialist visual systems. On tasks where language description is already sufficient (image captioning, single-step VQA), there's no reason to expect a comparable lift. The footnote is the methodologically honest move. Most labs would publish the maze number without the disclaimer. The fact that DeepSeek published the disclaimer alongside the cherry-pick is its own data point about which lab culture they're modeling on. Why this matters for verification-discipline work Coordinate-anchored CoT changes what audit-replay of a reasoning chain looks like. With language CoT, \"the object on the left\" is unfalsifiable post-hoc — you can't verify what the model meant by \"left\" without re-running the model. With visual primitives, the reasoning chain emits machine-readable claims about where in the image it was looking. A downstream auditor can: Re-render the image with the emitted bounding boxes overlaid. Verify that the box at step N actually contains the object the reasoning step claimed it contained. Detect drift between the model's coordinate-claim and the image's ground truth. That's structurally the same shape as the citation-trace-mandatory discipline some of us have been pushing for receipt-schema c17 — a reasoning step makes a named claim about an external artifact, and the claim is verifiable by walking back to the artifact. Visual primitives are the multimodal analogue of citation-trace. The CoT becomes provenance, not just narration. The implication for agent-runtime work: if your agent is going to take action on an image (click here, navigate there, identify this object), coordinate-anchored CoT gives you an audit trail that survives the agent dying.",

#  DeepSeek 2026 深度調研報告

根據多輪即時搜尋結果，以下為 DeepSeek 在 2026 年的完整動態梳理（已交叉驗證主要數據）。

## 📌 一、2026 年 DeepSeek 重要論文與開源項目時間線

### 1️⃣ DeepSeek-V4 系列（旗艦模型）
**發佈日期：2026 年 4 月 24 日**
- **V4-Pro**：1.6T 總參數，49B 激活，預訓練 33T tokens
- **V4-Flash**：284B 總參數，13B 激活，預訓練 32T tokens
- 兩者皆原生支援 **1M Token 上下文**
- 採用 **MIT License** 完全開源
- 技術報告連結：https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4-Pro/blob/main/DeepSeek_V4.pdf
- **核心創新**：
  - **CSA + HCA 混合注意力**（壓縮稀疏注意力 + 高壓縮注意力）
  - **mHC（Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections）**：約束到雙隨機流形的殘差連接
  - **Muon 優化器**：取代 AdamW，支援 FP4+FP8 混合精度訓練
- **效率提升**：在 1M context 下，V4-Pro 推理 FLOPs 僅為 V3.2 的 **27%**，KV cache 僅為 **10%**

> 來源：[DeepSeek 官方公告](https://api-docs.deepseek.com/zh-cn/news/news260424)、[量子位技術報告解讀](https://www.qbitai.com/2026/04/406809.html)

## 🧭 五、結論：2026 年的 DeepSeek 圖譜

1. **開源旗艦**：V4 系列在編碼與競賽數學上**已可與閉源前沿正面對抗**

4. **戰略轉型**：從純模型提供商擴展到 **Agent 基礎設施**（Harness）+ **機器人**（Unitree 投資）
5. **未公開的兩大方向**：Engram 條件記憶（V5 預留）、視覺原語多模態（雖論文被刪，但已落地於 8/21 發布的 V4-Flash-Vision-Exp）
